### 4103. [restrict](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/restrict) *(verb)* ^w-4103
- **UK:** `/rɪˈstrɪkt/` · **US:** `/rɪˈstrɪkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/restr/restrict__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/restr/restrict__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बाधित करना, सीमित करना, परिमित/सीमित करना
- **Examples:**
  - restrict something to something Speed is restricted to 30 mph in towns.
  - We restrict the number of students per class to ten.
  - restrict something Fog severely restricted visibility.
  - Having small children tends to restrict your freedom.

### 4104. [restriction](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/restriction) *(noun)* ^w-4104
- **UK:** `/rɪˈstrɪkʃn/` · **US:** `/rɪˈstrɪkʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/restr/restriction__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/restr/restriction__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सीमा, रोक, प्रतिबन्ध
- **Examples:**
  - import/speed/travel restrictions
  - restriction on something to impose/place a restriction on something
  - The government has agreed to lift restrictions on press freedom.
  - There are no restrictions on the amount of money you can withdraw.

### 4105. [resume](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/resume_1) *(verb)* ^w-4105
- **UK:** `/rɪˈzjuːm/` · **US:** `/rɪˈzuːm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resum/resume__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resum/resume__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** फिर आरम्भ करना, पुनः प्राप्त करना, संक्षेप तैयार करना
- **Examples:**
  - resume (something) to resume talks/negotiations
  - She resumed her career after an interval of six years.
  - The noise resumed, louder than before.
  - There is no sign of the peace talks resuming.

### 4389. [speculate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/speculate) *(verb)* ^w-4389
- **UK:** `/ˈspekjuleɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈspekjuleɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/spe/specu/speculate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/spe/specu/speculate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनुमान लगाना, अंदाज़  लगाना, सट्टा लगाना
- **Examples:**
  - speculate (about/on/as to something) We all speculated about the reasons for her resignation.
  - They were all speculating as to the identity of the stranger.
  - speculate why, how, etc… It is useless to speculate why he did it.
  - speculate that… We can speculate that the stone circles were used in some sort of pagan ceremony.

### 4390. [speculation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/speculation) *(noun)* ^w-4390
- **UK:** `/ˌspekjuˈleɪʃn/` · **US:** `/ˌspekjuˈleɪʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/spe/specu/speculation__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/spe/specu/speculation__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सट्टेबाज़ी, विचार, चिंतन
- **Examples:**
  - His private life is the subject of much speculation.
  - speculation that… There was widespread speculation that she was going to resign.
  - speculation about something Today's announcement ends months of speculation about the company's future.
  - speculation over something The president’s absence led to speculation over his health.

### 4432. [stimulate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/stimulate) *(verb)* ^w-4432
- **UK:** `/ˈstɪmjuleɪt/` · **US:** `/ˈstɪmjuleɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sti/stimu/stimulate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sti/stimu/stimulate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उत्तेजित करना, प्रोत्साहित करना, प्रेरित करना

### 4433. [stimulus](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/stimulus) *(noun)* ^w-4433
- **UK:** `/ˈstɪmjələs/` · **US:** `/ˈstɪmjələs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sti/stimu/stimulus__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sti/stimu/stimulus__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** प्रोत्साहन, उद्दीपन
- **Examples:**
  - stimulus for something Books provide children with ideas and a stimulus for play.
  - stimulus to something The new tax laws should act as a stimulus to exports.
  - stimulus (for somebody) (to do something) This development also acted as a stimulus for Britain to modernize her air force.

- **UK:** `/ˈtestɪfaɪ/` · **US:** `/ˈtestɪfaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tes/testi/testify__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tes/testi/testify__us_1.mp3)

### 4813. [unless](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/unless) *(conjunction)* ^w-4813
